Let's design final program modules:

Utility:
- next_power_of_two
- fast_self_conv(a): FFT length 2n-1 next power, rfft a, square, irfft. Use nan_to_num.
- corr_pair(a, w): Compute h_j = Σ_k w_k a_{k-j} valid. Use FFT if active dense or n moderate. But to support n=16384 with w length 2n-1, use FFT length next_power(n + len(w)-1) = 3n-2. Could be 65536. Fine.
  Implementation: ar = a[::-1]; full = ifft? Use rfft: A = rfft(ar, N), W = rfft(w, N); full=irfft(A*W,N)[:M]; return full[n-1:2n-1]. Check indices. For a length n, w length L=2n-1. M=n+L-1=3n-2. full[m] = Σ_i ar_i w_{m-i}. We need h_j at m=n-1+j: Σ_k w_k ar_{m-k}=Σ_k w_k a_{n-1-(n-1+j-k)}=Σ_k w_k a_{k-j}. Good.
- normalize_a(a): clip negative/nan, sum, if sum<1e-12 uniform, return a/S.
- exact_score(a): n, S, c, cmax, return 2n cmax/S² (or inf). Ensure max 0 for small S.

Implement lse_normalized_grad(y, beta):
  y = clip(y - max(y), -50, 50) to avoid overflow; a = exp(y); S=a.sum(); a/=S.
  c=fast_self_conv(a)
  cmax = max(c)
  z=beta*(c-cmax); clip; e=exp(z); sume; w=e/sume; L=cmax + log(sume)/beta
  F=2*n*L (constant factor)
  h=corr_pair(a,w) # no factor
  gradq=2*h
  dot = sum(a*gradq)=2 sum(a*h)
  grad_y = a*gradq - a*dot = 2*a*(h - sum(a*h))
  PROJECT: grad_y -= grad_y.mean()
  return F, grad_y

def spectral_square_root(c, n, phase_fix=True):
  # c length 2n-1 nonnegative sum maybe 1
  N=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
  C=np.fft.rfft(c, N)
  # Ensure C nonnegative? Complex.
  # Use sqrt with phase continuous? Maybe take log for nonzero:
  # A = np.exp(0.5*np.log(C))? np.log complex gives principal.
  A = np.sqrt(C)  # principal
  # A[0] should be real nonnegative (since c sum positive maybe). If A[0] real small negative due round, set abs.
  if not np.isfinite(A[0]) or A[0] < 0: A[0]=abs(A[0])
  a = np.fft.irfft(A, N)[:n]
  # remove negative by soft clip? maybe a = np.clip(a,0,None)
  return a

Then try c candidates. The resulting a may have length n, endpoints? Check sum. If score good, accept. This is O(FFT) per candidate, cheap. We can random/generate many (hundreds) in a phase. It may find new basins.

Let's outline final algorithm in code:

Helpers:

Core optimizers:

1. best_score_sequence(a, deadline, verbose): Perhaps best local routine that combines mirror_adam and LSE-LBFGS.
But too many phases inside.

Detailed phase implementations:

A. mirror_optimize(a, deadline, tag, verbose):
Inputs a normalized. Returns best_sc,best_a.
State: tau = 20.0 maybe, lr=0.2, m,v arrays? Let's use adaptive subgradient without ADAM first, then maybe ADAM variant. There are two update families:
   - Soft weights softmax ratio.
   - VR weights.
Pseudo:
  a=normalize; best=a; best_sc=exact; n; t0; m=zeros(n); v=zeros(n); beta1,beta2=0.9,0.999; step=0; t0...
  tau=50; lr=0.2; stagnant=0; smooth_phase=0...
  for it in range(until deadline):
    c=fast_self_conv(a); cmax=max; sc=2n*cmax; update best.
    # choose weights mode: alternate tau values.
    w=softmax_ratio(c,tau)
    h=corr_pair(a,w)
    # equalize h: subtract mean; scale by (max-min) or median abs.
    h_center = h - np.mean(h)
    h_scale = max(np.max(h_center)-np.min(h_center), 1e-12)
    # Adam moments on g=h_center/h_scale
    g = h_center / h_scale
    m = beta1*m + (1-beta1)*g
    v = beta2*v + (1-beta2)*g*g
    m_hat=m/(1-beta1**step); v_hat=v/(1-beta2**step)
    base_delta = -lr * m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+1e-8)
    # Try multipliers: 0.25,0.5,1,1.5 (maybe 2)
    best_iter_sc=sc; best_a=a; best_mult=1
    for mult in [0.25,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0]:
       delta=clip(base_delta*mult, -8,8)
       a_try=normalize(a*np.exp(delta))
       s_try=exact_score(a_try)
       if s_try<best_iter_sc: update
    # Also try plain subgradient no Adam with sub_etas? maybe if ADAM fails.
    # Also variance reduction
    if VR condition:
       top_m=...; vc=...; wvr=excess; hv=corr; center scale; a_try=normalize(a*exp(-lr_vr*gvr)); evaluate.
    if best_iter_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
       a=best_a; best_sc; stagnant=0; lr*=1.05? maybe up to 1.0
    else:
       stagnant++; lr*=0.85? tau adjust
    # Adapt tau: cycle [10,50,200,1000,5000,20000] or if stagnant>20 change tau, lr.
    # Occasionally perturb (itr%100==0) with small random to escape: m,v reset.
  return best.

This uses ADAM on centered h. At high tau, could oscillate. Maybe use simple step if ADAM no improve. We can also use exact h weights top_k to reduce noise.

B. subgrad_sweep(a, deadline): Like previous but better weights. For top_k/tau combos. Since mirror_adam maybe enough, but sweep can polish. Use active weights uniform top-k and softmax tau. For each combo, step with few lrs. Keep best. Time limited.

  Call minimize(lse_softmax_objective, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(tau,), options). tau dynamic. Objective returns F=2n L (where L LSE in normalized c?) and grad_y projected. Need top-level function.

  Function top-level:

def softmax_lse_objective(y, tau):
   y = np.asarray(y, dtype=float)
   # gauge: center y by subtract max? But for softmax, subtract max for stability; objective invariant if we subtract mean? L-BFGS variables not gauge invariant? If we center by max, gradient projection needed. Better not modify y except clip: y = np.clip(y, -50, 50); a=np.exp(y); S=a.sum(); a /= S. But adding constant changes y but not a; objective gauge invariant; if we don't center, L-BFGS may drift but okay. Numerical if y large, exp overflow. We can center by subtract max inside and return projected gradient. Let's do stable:
   ymax = np.max(y); yc = y - ymax; yc = np.clip(yc, -50, 50); a = np.exp(yc); S=a.sum(); a/=S.
   But if we change y to yc, objective as function of y is not differentiable at max? But softmax is smooth; using max subtract is just numerically equivalent to exp(y)/sum for finite. The gradient wrt original y equals gradient wrt yc projected? Since yc = y - max(y). For y, derivative of yc has P max relation. softmax a depends only on y differences. We can compute gradient wrt y using yc gradient then project out ones: Because function of y is F(yc(y)). yc gradient = P grad_yc where P=I - 11^T/n *? Specifically d( y-max(y) )/dy = I - u_i? Let e_i max? If multiple max, subgradient. Simpler: Just return gradient computed via a*(h-dot) and then subtract its mean to remove gauge. Implement ratio_weights:
def ratio_weights(c, tau, top_cap=None):
  cmax=np.max(c)
  if cmax<=1e-300: return np.ones(len(c))/len(c)
  z=tau*(c-cmax)/cmax
  z=np.clip(z, -50.0, 0.0)
  e=np.exp(z)
  s=e.sum()
  if s<=0: uniform
  w=e/s
  if top_cap is not None and w nonzeros >top_cap? Could zero tiny: w[w<1e-15]=0 normalize. Not needed.

excess_weights from top_m:
def excess_weights(c, top_m=20):
  cmax=max; if <=0 uniform
  # top values index via argpartition
  k=min(top_m, len(c))
  idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
  topc=c[idx]
  mean_top=topc.mean()
  excess=np.maximum(c-mean_top,0)
  s=excess.sum()
  if s<=1e-300: return uniform top
  return excess/s

Mirror function:
def multiplicative_update(a, h, lr, scale=None):
  h=h - np.mean(h)
  if scale is None: scale=max(h.max()-h.min(),1e-12)
  delta=-lr*h/scale
  delta=np.clip(delta, -10,10)
  out=a*np.exp(delta)
  return normalize_a(out)

So objective function top-level:
def softmax_lse_objective(y, beta):
  y=center? compute a softmax stable.
  c=fast_self_conv(a)
  d=n*c (S=1)
  dmax=np.max(d)
  z=beta*(d-dmax) clip
  e=exp(z); sume; w=e/sum
  L=dmax + log(sume)/beta
  F=2*n*L  # = score smooth
  h=corr_pair(a,w)
  adot=sum(a*h)
  grad=4*n*a*(h-adot)
  grad -= grad.mean()
  return float(F), grad

spectral_square_root(c, n):
  m=len(c) # 2n-1
  N=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
  C=np.fft.rfft(c, N)
  # To choose branch, set angle continuous? Let's try root = np.sqrt(C * np.sqrt? no)
  # Use principal sqrt but enforce A[0] positive real.
  A=np.sqrt(C)
  # If imaginary part at A[0] nonzero, take real if real positive else abs.
  if abs(A[0].imag) > abs(A[0].real)*0.1:
      A[0]=complex(abs(A[0].real),0.0)
  else:
      A[0]=complex(abs(A[0].real),0.0)
  # Ensure Hermitian? rfft array corresponds nonnegative half; irfft handles. But due numerical branch may not satisfy A[0] and Nyquist real? Set A[-1]=real if len>1? For N even, Nyquist must real. A[-1]=A[-1].real
  a=np.fft.irfft(A,N)[:n]
  # a may have small imaginary? irfft real.
  return a

next_power_of_two:
```
def next_power_of_two(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

fast_self_conv:
```
def fast_self_conv(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    N=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
```
Since A*A complex; if a real, A*A Hermitian. Good.

corr_pair:
```
def corr_pair(a,w):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float); w=np.asarray(w,dtype=float)
    n=a.size; L=w.size
    if n==0 or L==0: return empty
    M=n+L-1; N=next_power_of_two(M)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,N)
    full=np.fft.irfft(A*W,N)[:M]
    return full[n-1:n+n]
```
If w length maybe not 2n-1? It should be len c. But for top weights maybe same length. Good. For w shorter (excess full length) okay. If w length m not 2n-1? corr formula for general L: h_j = Σ_k w_k a_{k-j} valid if k range 0..L-1. Extraction full[n-1:2n-1] works for L=2n-1. If L different, M=n+L-1; we need h_j for j=0..n-1, m=n-1+j; as long as m<M. For k-j valid. But if L less than 2n-1 maybe some w zeros; okay. For L greater? not. Ensure w len==2n-1.

normalize_a:
```
def normalize_a(a):
  a=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel(), nan=0.0,posinf=1e6,neginf=0.0)
  a=np.clip(a,0,None)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<1e-300: return np.full(a.size,1.0/max(1,a.size))
  return a/s
```

exact_score:
```
def exact_score(a, conv=None):
  n=len(a)
  if n==0: return inf
  S=float(np.sum(a))
  if S<1e-15: return inf
  if conv is None: conv=fast_self_conv(a)
  cmax=float(np.max(conv)) if conv.size else 0
  if cmax<0: cmax=0
  return 2*n*cmax/(S*S)
```

ratio_weights_from_e:
```
def ratio_weights(e,beta):
  e=np.asarray(e)
  emax=e.max()
  if emax<=1e-300: return np.ones(e.size)/e.size
  z=beta*(e-emax)
  z=np.clip(z,-50,0)
  expz=np.exp(z)
  s=expz.sum()
  if s<=0: uniform
  w=expz/s
  # maybe zero tiny
  w[w<1e-18]=0; s2=sum; if s2>0 w/=s2 else uniform
  return w
```
Use e=2n*c. Beta dimensionless.

excess_weights(c, top_m):
```
def excess_weights(c, top_m):
  c=np.asarray(c)
  m=c.size
  if m==0: return empty
  dmax=c.max()
  if dmax<=... return uniform
  k=min(max(1,top_m), m)
  idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
  mean_top=c[idx].mean()
  excess=np.maximum(c-mean_top,0.0)
  s=excess.sum()
  if s<=...:
     w=np.zeros(m); w[idx]=1/k; return w
  return excess/s
```

multiplicative_update:
```
def multiplicative_update(a,h,lr):
  h=np.asarray(h)
  base=np.mean(h)
  hc=h-base
  scale=float(np.max(hc)-np.min(hc))
  if scale<1e-300: return a.copy()
  delta=-lr*hc/scale
  delta=np.clip(delta,-10,10)
  out=a*np.exp(delta)
  return normalize_a(out)
```

Adam mirror: Need top-level? We can inline in mirror_optimize, but local function no? Variables local okay. No closures. We need maybe a helper to normalize y and compute softmax.

softmax_normalize:
```
def softmax_normalize(y):
  y=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(y,dtype=float), nan=-50,posinf=50,neginf=-50)
  yc=y-np.max(y)
  yc=np.clip(yc,-50,0) # can keep not normalized exactly?
  a=np.exp(yc)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=0: return np.full(y.size,1/y.size)
  return a/s
```
Clipping negative to -50 may distort if y have large spread; but okay. Use -40? If want zeros, -50. For gradient, if many at -50, a tiny. Fine.

softmax_lse_objective(y,beta):
```
def softmax_lse_objective(y,beta):
  y=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(y,dtype=float), nan=-50.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
  ymax=np.max(y)
  yc=y-ymax
  yc=np.clip(yc,-50.0,0.0)
  a=np.exp(yc)
  s=a.sum()
  if s<=0 or not finite: a=np.full(y.size,1/y.size)
  else: a/=s
  n=y.size
  c=fast_self_conv(a)
  e=2.0*n*c
  emax=float(np.max(e))
  if emax<=1e-300:
      return 0.0, np.zeros(n)
  z=beta*(e-emax)
  z=np.clip(z,-50.0,0.0)
  expz=np.exp(z)
  ssum=expz.sum()
  if ssum<=0 or not finite:
      w=np.ones(e.size)/e.size
      L=emax
  else:
      w=expz/ssum
      L=emax + math.log(ssum)/beta
  h=corr_pair(a,w)
  adot=float(np.dot(a,h))
  grad=4.0*n*a*(h-adot)
  grad=np.nan_to_num(grad,nan=0,posinf=0,neginf=0)
  grad=grad - np.mean(grad)
  return float(L), grad
```
Is L = LSE_e approx score? e=2n c, L≈emax=score. F= L (not 2). Good. In L-BFGS options, objective values ~1.5.
But L-BFGS minimizing LSE_e. Gradient formula: F = LSE_e. ∂F/∂a = 2n * ∑ w_k ∂c_k? Wait e=2n c. LSE_e = (1/β)logΣexp(β e_k). derivative wrt e =β, times weights: w·∂e/∂a. ∂e=2n ∂c =4n h? Wait ∂c/∂a =2h (h from corr of w? w weights on e same as c; h=Σw a; derivative wrt a =2 Σw a_{k-j}=2h). Then ∂e/∂a=2n*2h=4n h. So grad_a=4n h. Good. Tangent wrt y: grad_y_i= a_i grad_a_i - a_i Σ a_j grad_a_j =4n a_i h_i -4n a_i Σ a_j h_j. Correct. L = emax + logsumexp(β(e-emax))/β. Derivative wrt e = w (since LSE derivative). Yes.

Mirror weights use e=2n*c and beta. Good.

In lse_lbfgs_run, after minimize res.x, compute a=softmax_normalize(res.x). But if y very negative all, softmax ok. Evaluate exact.

    stagnant=0
    it=0

        it += 1
        c=fast_self_conv(a)
        emax=float(np.max(2*n*c? avoid copy) e=2*n*c maybe create array.

def resample_sequence(a,n):
  a=...; m=len(a)
  if m==n return a
  x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)

def log_probs_from_a(a):
  a=normalize_a(a)
  floor=1e-12/max(1,len(a))
  a2=np.maximum(a,floor)
  a2=a2/a2.sum()
  y=np.log(a2)
  y-=np.mean(y)
  return y
```

def normalize_floor(a, floor=1e-15):
   out=normalize_a(a)
   m=out.size
   f=floor/m
   out=np.maximum(out,f)
   out/=out.sum()
   return out

def mirror_optimize(a, deadline, rng=None, verbose=True, tag=""):
    a=normalize_floor(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=exact_score(a)
    n=a.size
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); step=0
    beta1=0.85; beta2=0.99
    lr=0.25; lr_min=0.01; lr_max=2.0
    taus=np.array([1.0,5,10,20,50,100,200,500,1000,2000,5000,10000,20000],dtype=float)
    tau_idx=3 #20
    stagnant=0; it=0; no_improve_window=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       it+=1
       c=fast_self_conv(a)
       e=2.0*n*c
       emax=float(np.max(e))
       sc=emax
       if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
          best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
          if verbose and it%100==0: print...
       w=ratio_weights(e, taus[tau_idx])
       h=corr_pair(a,w)
       hc=h - float(np.mean(h))
       hscale=...
       if hscale < 1e-22:
           stagnant += 5
       else:
           g=hc/hscale
           step += 1
           dec1=1.0-(1.0-beta1)**step? Better use bias correction b1=1-beta1**step. Computing beta1**step ok.
           m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)*g
           v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)*g*g
           b1=1.0-beta1**step
           b2=1.0-beta2**step
           base_delta=-lr*m/(b1*(np.sqrt(v/b2)+1e-8))? Wait m_hat=m/b1, v_hat=v/b2. So base_delta=-lr*m_hat/(sqrt(v_hat)+eps). We'll implement.
           best_iter_sc=sc; best_iter_a=a
           # Adam mults
           for mult in (0.25,0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0):
              a_try=multiplicative_delta_update(a, base_delta*mult)
              sc_try=exact_score(a_try)
              if sc_try<best_iter_sc: ...
           # If no improve over current and maybe after 5 iterations, try plain centered with eta list
           if best_iter_sc >= sc - 1e-13:
              for eta in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
                 a_try=multiplicative_delta_update(a, -eta*hc/hscale)
                 sc_try=exact_score(a_try)
                 if sc_try<best_iter_sc: ...
              # VR
              top_m = int(min(max(20, n//32), 200))
              wvr=excess_weights(c, top_m)
              hv=corr_pair(a,wvr)
              hvc=hv - np.mean(hv)
              hvscale=max(max-min)
              if hvscale>...:
                 for eta in (0.05,0.1,0.2):
                    a_try=...
           if best_iter_sc < sc - 1e-13:
              a=best_iter_a
              # adapt lr
              if best_mult <1: lr=lr*0.9
              else: lr=min(lr_max,lr*1.02)
              stagnant=0
              tau_idx=min(len(taus)-1, tau_idx + (1 if it%200==0 else 0))
           else:
              stagnant +=1
              lr=max(lr_min, lr*0.9)
              if stagnant>15:
                 tau_idx=(tau_idx+1)%len(taus)
                 stagnant=8
              if stagnant>60 and rng is not None:
                 p=perturb(a,rng,strength=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.2)))
                 # Set working a to p, reset moments, but keep best
                 a=normalize_floor(p)
                 m.fill(0); v.fill(0); step=0
                 stagnant=0
                 lr=min(lr*1.2, lr_max)
                 tau_idx=max(0, tau_idx-1)
       # safety break if time
    return best_sc,best_a
```

def active_subgrad_sweep(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag=""):
  a=normalize_floor(a); best=a; sc=exact; n; 
  # maybe only 2 rounds
  for round in range(3):
    if time > deadline: break
    c=fast_self_conv(best); e=2n c
    weight_list=[]
    for beta in [5,20,100,1000,10000,100000]: weight_list.append((ratio_weights(e,beta), 'beta'+str(beta)))
    for k in [1,3,10,50]: weight_list.append((top_uniform_weights(c,k), ...))
    weight_list.append((excess_weights(c, min(50,n//16)), 'vr'))
    improved=False
    for w,name in weight_list:
       h=corr_pair(best,w); hc=h-mean; hscale=max-min
       if hscale<=: continue
       for eta in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2]:
          a_try=multiplicative_delta_update(best, -eta*hc/hscale)
          sc_try=exact_score(a_try)
          if sc_try<best_sc-1e-13: update best...
    if no improve break
  return
```

Now, spherical square root candidates function:
```
def spectral_square_root(c, n):
    m=2*n-1
    N=next_power_of_two(m)
    c_padded = np.asarray(c,dtype=float)
    if c_padded.size < m: pad

def lse_lbfgs_run(a, deadline, verbose=True, tag=""):
    best_a=normalize_floor(a); best_sc=exact_score(best_a)
    if _scipy_minimize is None: return best_sc,best_a
    n=len(best_a)
    y=log_probs_from_a(best_a)
    beta_list=[30,60,120,250,500,1000,2000,4000,8000,15000]
    # estimate dt
    t=monotonic(); F,g=softmax_lse_objective(y, beta_list[0]); dt=monotonic()-t; dt=max(dt,0.02)
    for beta in beta_list:
       rem=deadline - time.monotonic() - 5
       if rem<20: break
       maxfun=int(min(700, max(30, rem / max(dt,0.02))))
       maxiter=max(20, min(300, maxfun//4))
       opts={'maxfun':maxfun, 'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':1e-13, 'gtol':1e-9, 'maxcor':20, 'disp':0}
       try:

                if verbose: print
       except Exception:
          pass
       # if y from res, maybe use for next beta rather than best? Use best.
    return best_sc,best_a
```

`normalize_floor` needs optional floor:
```
def normalize_floor(a, rel_floor=1e-15):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0: return a
    f = rel_floor / m
    out = np.maximum(a, f)
    s = out.sum()
    if s <= 0: return a
    return out / s
```
Rel_floor 1e-15/m -> total floor 1e-15. Good.

`multiplicative_delta_update(a, delta)`:
```
def multiplicative_delta_update(a, delta):
    delta=np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(delta,dtype=float), nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    delta=np.clip(delta, -12.0, 12.0)
    out=a*np.exp(delta)
    return normalize_floor(out, 1e-15)
```

`log_probs_from_a`:
```
def log_probs_from_a(a):
    a=normalize_a(a)
    n=a.size
    f=1e-12/max(1,n)
    a2=np.maximum(a, f)
    s=a2.sum()
    if s<=0: y=np.zeros(n)
    else: y=np.log(a2/s)
    y=y-np.mean(y)
    return np.nan_to_num(y, nan=-30.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
```

`softmax_normalize` uses -30? Let's use `EXP_CLIP= -35.0`? Define top-level constant `EXP_CLIP = -35.0`.
```
def softmax_normalize(y):
   y=np.nan_to_num(...)
   if y.size==0: return empty
   ymax=np.max(y)
   yc=y-ymax
   yc=np.clip(yc, EXP_CLIP, 0.0)
   a=np.exp(yc)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(y.size,1/y.size)
   a/=s
   # floor? no; for optimization maybe use normalize_floor outside.
   return a
```
`EXP_CLIP = -35.0`.

def spectral_flatten_candidates(a, base_sc, deadline, rng, verbose=True):
    a0=normalize_floor(a); local_a=a0.copy(); local_sc=exact_score(a0)
    n=a0.size; m=2*n-1
    c=fast_self_conv(a0); c=np.nan_to_num(c); if sum>0 c/=sum
    cmax=float(np.max(c)); base=np.ones(m)/m
    count=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2 and count < 1000:
       count+=1
       mode=int(rng.integers(0,8))
       c_new = None
       if mode==0:
          t=cmax*float(rng.uniform(0.90,0.995))
          c_new=flatten_c(c,t)
       elif mode==1:
          t=cmax*float(rng.uniform(0.95,0.999)); c_new=flatten_c(c,t); alpha=rng.uniform(0.1,0.4); c_new=(1-alpha)c_new+alpha*base
       elif mode==2:
          alpha=rng.uniform(0.2,0.8); c_new=(1-alpha)*c+alpha*base
       elif mode==3:
          k=int(rng.integers(11,51)); if even++; sig=k/4; kern=...; c_new=np.convolve(c,kern,'same')
       elif mode==4:
          x=...; arr=np.exp(kappa*(2x-1)+...); c_new=arr
       elif mode==5:
          freq=int(rng.integers(1,4)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)); c_new=1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x)+0.1
       elif mode==6:
          # current with random noise
          c_new=c*(1+0.05*rng.standard_normal(m)); c_new=np.maximum(c_new,1e-18)
       else:
          t=cmax*float(rng.uniform(0.98,0.999)); c_new=flatten_c(c,t)
       c_new=np.nan_to_num(c_new,nan=0,posinf=0,neginf=0); c_new=np.maximum(c_new,1e-20); s=c_new.sum(); if s<=0 continue; c_new/=s
       raw=spectral_square_root(c_new,n)
       for shift in (0.0, -float(np.min(raw)) if np.any(np.min(raw))<0 else 0.0):? Simpler:
          arr1=np.clip(raw,0,None)
          arr2=raw - np.min(raw) + 1e-12
       for arr in (arr1,arr2):
          if sum>...:
             a_try=normalize_floor(arr); sc=exact_score(a_try); if sc<local_sc: update
       if local_sc < base_sc and count%50==0: print
    return local_sc, local_a
```
Note: `base_sc` passed maybe lower than local initial? If base_sc is global parent 1.506 and local initial high length 1.51, local_sc >base. target coefficient using cmax from local; ok.
`flatten_c` top-level:
```
def flatten_c(c, t):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=float)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    over=np.maximum(c-t,0.0)
    out=c-over
    deficit=1.0 - out.sum()
    if deficit>0:
       out=out + deficit/c.size
    else:
       # if deficit <=0, reduce all under t proportionally?
       if deficit < -1e-12:
          under=np.maximum(t-out,0.0)
          us=under.sum()
          if us>0:
             out=out - (-deficit)*under/us
          else:
             out=out - (-deficit)/c.size
       # floor
    out=np.maximum(out,1e-20)
    out/=out.sum()
    return out
```
If t small, over sum could exceed 1? out sum ≥0, deficit≤1. ok.

Spectral square root of c_new with flush may produce a with sum not 1. We clip/normalize.

Now, `active_subgrad_sweep`: We need top_uniform_weights. Sigma:
```
def active_subgrad_sweep(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag=""):
  a=normalize_floor(a); best=a; sc=exact
  n=a.size; rounds=0; max_rounds=4
  while rounds<max_rounds and time<deadline-2:
     rounds+=1; c=fast_self_conv(best); e=2n c
     weight_list=[]
     for beta in (2,10,50,300,2000,20000):
        weight_list.append(ratio_weights(e,beta))
     for k in (1,3,8,24,80):
        weight_list.append(top_uniform_weights(c,k))
     weight_list.append(excess_weights(c, min(64,max(16,n//64))))
     improved=False
     for w in weight_list:
       h=corr_pair(best,w); hc=h-mean; hscale=...
       if hscale<...: continue
       for eta in (0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2):
          a_try=multiplicative_delta_update(best,-eta*hc/hscale)
          sc_try=exact_score(a_try)
          if sc_try<sc-1e-13: sc=sc_try; best=a_try; improved=True
     if not improved: break
  return sc,best
```
This can be called on final length; if length 16384, weight_list ~12, corr 12 + candidate 60 conv; maybe 0.2s per round. fine.

One more thought: Objective in L-BFGS uses `e=2n c`; for smooth LSE with beta, if beta finite, LSE values around score. For exact score, max e = score. Weighted gradient corresponds to LSE. Good.

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative sequence minimizing

    2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / (sum(a))^2

using a hierarchical, multi-resolution local-optimization pipeline:

1. Start from the supplied parent sequence if available; otherwise use a small
   gallery of smooth / skewed / periodic initial shapes.
2. Optimize at a lower resolution (usually near the parent length) using
   adaptive exponentiated-gradient mirror descent and LSE-L-BFGS.
3. Upsample the best low-resolution result to a larger length and repeat the
   same tools.
4. Use spectral square-root projections of flattened convolution targets as a
   diversification mechanism.
5. Finish with short perturb/reoptimize moves and a deterministic active-set
   subgradient polish.

The key local objects are:
- exact score via FFT self-convolution,
- active weights from a log-sum-exp approximation of the maximum,
- multiplicative updates in log-space driven by correlation pressure,
- an LSE smooth objective optimized with L-BFGS in softmax/log-domain.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _scipy_minimize
except Exception:
    _scipy_minimize = None

_EXP_CLIP = -35.0


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_a(a):
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(),
                      nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0 / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def normalize_floor(a, rel_floor=1e-15):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return a
    f = rel_floor / float(m)
    out = np.maximum(a, f)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0:
        return a
    return out / s


def fast_self_conv(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = 2 * n - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    out = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:M]
    return np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def corr_pair(a, w):
    """
    Returns h_j = sum_k w_k a_{k-j} whenever 0 <= k-j < len(a).
    This is the un-doubled derivative kernel for self-convolution.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(w.size)
    if n == 0 or L == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    M = n + L - 1
    N = next_power_of_two(M)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    full = np.fft.irfft(A * W, N)[:M]
    return full[n - 1:n + n]


def exact_score(a, conv=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-15:
        return np.inf
    if conv is None:
        conv = fast_self_conv(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(conv)) if conv.size else 0.0
    if cmax < 0:
        cmax = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cmax / (S * S))


def ratio_weights(e, beta):
    """
    Softmax-like weights on active convolution positions.
    e is typically 2*n*conv(a,a), whose maximum approximates the score.
    """
    e = np.asarray(e, dtype=np.float64)
    m = e.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    emax = float(np.max(e))
    if emax <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    z = beta * (e - emax)
    z = np.clip(z, _EXP_CLIP, 0.0)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    w = ez / s
    w = np.nan_to_num(w, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    w[w < 1e-18] = 0.0
    s2 = float(w.sum())
    if s2 <= 0:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s2


def top_uniform_weights(c, k):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    k = int(max(1, min(k, m)))
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def excess_weights(c, top_m):
    """
    Weight proportional to excess of convolution values over the mean of a top set.
    Useful for directly equalizing the largest peaks.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = c.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
    top_m = int(max(1, min(top_m, m)))
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_m)[-top_m:]
    mean_top = float(np.mean(c[idx]))
    excess = np.maximum(c - mean_top, 0.0)
    s = float(excess.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(top_m)
        return w
    return excess / s


def multiplicative_delta_update(a, delta):
    delta = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(delta, dtype=np.float64),
                          nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    delta = np.clip(delta, -12.0, 12.0)
    out = a * np.exp(delta)
    return normalize_floor(out, 1e-15)


def softmax_normalize(y):
    y = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64),
                      nan=-50.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    ymax = float(np.max(y))
    yc = y - ymax
    yc = np.clip(yc, _EXP_CLIP, 0.0)
    a = np.exp(yc)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(y.size, 1.0 / float(y.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def log_probs_from_a(a):
    a = normalize_a(a)
    n = a.size
    f = 1e-12 / max(1, n)
    a2 = np.maximum(a, f)
    s = float(a2.sum())
    if s <= 0:
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        y = np.log(a2 / s)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=-30.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
    y = y - float(np.mean(y))
    return y


def softmax_lse_objective(y, beta):
    """
    Smooth score objective in softmax/log-domain.

    Objective is an LSE approximation to 2*n*max(conv(a,a)) with sum(a)=1.
    Gradient is the corresponding active-weight score gradient projected
    onto the affine tangent space of the softmax reparametrization.
    """
    y = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64),
                      nan=-50.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    n = y.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0, np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)

    ymax = float(np.max(y))
    yc = y - ymax
    yc = np.clip(yc, _EXP_CLIP, 0.0)
    a = np.exp(yc)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / s

    c = fast_self_conv(a)
    e = 2.0 * float(n) * c
    if e.size == 0:
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    emax = float(np.max(e))
    if emax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(emax):
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    if beta <= 1e-12:
        w = np.full(e.size, 1.0 / float(e.size), dtype=np.float64)
        L = float(np.mean(e))
    else:
        z = beta * (e - emax)
        z = np.clip(z, _EXP_CLIP, 0.0)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        ssum = float(ez.sum())
        if ssum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ssum):
            w = np.full(e.size, 1.0 / float(e.size), dtype=np.float64)
            L = emax
        else:
            w = ez / ssum
            L = emax + math.log(ssum) / float(beta)

    h = corr_pair(a, w)
    adot = float(np.dot(a, h))
    grad = 4.0 * float(n) * a * (h - adot)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))

    if not np.isfinite(L):
        return 1e10, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(L), grad


def lse_lbfgs_run(a, deadline, verbose=True, tag=""):
    """
    LSE-L-BFGS refinement in log-domain.  Uses several increasing beta values.
    """
    best_a = normalize_floor(a)
    best_sc = exact_score(best_a)
    n = best_a.size
    if _scipy_minimize is None or n == 0:
        return float(best_sc), best_a

    y = log_probs_from_a(best_a)
    beta_list = [30, 60, 120, 250, 500, 1000, 2000, 4000, 8000, 15000]

    # One representative call to estimate per-evaluation cost.
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        _, _ = softmax_lse_objective(y, beta_list[0])
        dt = max(time.monotonic() - t0, 0.02)
    except Exception:
        dt = 0.05

    for beta in beta_list:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0
        if rem < 20.0:
            break
        maxfun = int(min(700, max(30, rem / max(dt, 0.02))))
        maxiter = int(max(20, min(300, maxfun // 4)))
        opts = {
            "maxfun": maxfun,
            "maxiter": maxiter,
            "ftol": 1e-13,
            "gtol": 1e-9,
            "maxcor": 20,
            "disp": 0,
        }
        try:
            res = _scipy_minimize(
                softmax_lse_objective,
                y,
                jac=True,
                args=(float(beta),),
                method="L-BFGS",
                options=opts,
            )
            a_try = normalize_floor(softmax_normalize(res.x))
            sc = exact_score(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc * 1.005:
                y = log_probs_from_a(a_try)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
                best_sc = float(sc)
                best_a = a_try.copy()
                if verbose:
                    print("  [{} lse-b] beta={:8.1f} maxfun={:5d} score={:.10f}".format(
                        tag, beta, maxfun, best_sc))
        except Exception:
            continue
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def mirror_optimize(a, deadline, rng=None, verbose=True, tag="", tau_idx=3):
    """
    Adaptive exponentiated-gradient / ADAM-style mirror update on the simplex.
    Uses active LSE weights, plain pressure, and top-peak excess equalization.
    """
    a = normalize_floor(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0 or not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        return np.inf, a

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    step = 0
    beta1 = 0.85
    beta2 = 0.99

    lr = 0.25
    lr_min = 0.01
    lr_max = 2.5

    taus = np.array([1.0, 5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 50.0, 100.0, 200.0,
                     500.0, 1000.0, 2000.0, 5000.0, 10000.0, 20000.0],
                    dtype=np.float64)
    idx = int(max(0, min(tau_idx, len(taus) - 1)))
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = np.inf

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        it += 1

        c = fast_self_conv(a)
        e = 2.0 * float(n) * c
        sc = float(np.max(e)) if e.size else np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-13:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            if verbose and (it % 400 == 0 or best_sc < last_print - 1e-9):
                print("  [{} mirror it={:5d}] score={:.10f} tau={:.2f} lr={:.4f}".format(
                    tag, it, best_sc, taus[idx], lr))
                last_print = best_sc

        w = ratio_weights(e, float(taus[idx]))
        h = corr_pair(a, w)
        if h.size != n:
            break

        hc = h - float(np.mean(h))
        hc = np.nan_to_num(hc, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        hscale = float(np.max(hc) - np.min(hc))

        if hscale > 1e-25:
            g = np.clip(hc / hscale, -5.0, 5.0)
            step += 1
            m = beta1 * m + (1.0 - beta1) * g
            v = beta2 * v + (1.0 - beta2) * (g * g)
            m = np.nan_to_num(m, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

            b1 = 1.0 - (beta1 ** step)
            b2 = 1.0 - (beta2 ** step)
            if b1 <= 1e-14 or b2 <= 1e-14:
                base_delta = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                mhat = m / b1
                vhat = v / b2
                base_delta = -lr * mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + 1e-8)
            base_delta = np.nan_to_num(base_delta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

            best_iter_sc = sc
            best_iter_a = a
            best_mult = 1.0

            # Adam-family line search.
            for mult in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
                a_try = multiplicative_delta_update(a, base_delta * mult)
                sc_try = exact_score(a_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_iter_sc - 1e-13:
                    best_iter_sc = sc_try
                    best_iter_a = a_try
                    best_mult = mult

            # Plain centered pressure, sometimes escapes from bad Adam scaling.
            if best_iter_sc >= sc - 1e-13:
                for eta in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4):
                    a_try = multiplicative_delta_update(a, -eta * hc / hscale)
                    sc_try = exact_score(a_try)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_iter_sc - 1e-13:
                        best_iter_sc = sc_try
                        best_iter_a = a_try
                        best_mult = eta

                # Top-peak excess equalization.
                top_m = int(min(max(20, n // 64), 300))
                wvr = excess_weights(c, top_m)
                hv = corr_pair(a, wvr)
                hvc = hv - float(np.mean(hv))
                hvc = np.nan_to_num(hvc, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
                hvs = float(np.max(hvc) - np.min(hvc))
                if hvs > 1e-25:
                    for eta in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2):
                        a_try = multiplicative_delta_update(a, -eta * hvc / hvs)
                        sc_try = exact_score(a_try)
                        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_iter_sc - 1e-13:
                            best_iter_sc = sc_try
                            best_iter_a = a_try
                            best_mult = eta

            if best_iter_sc < sc - 1e-13:
                a = best_iter_a
                stagnant = 0
                if best_mult < 1.0:
                    lr = max(lr_min, lr * 0.88)
                else:
                    lr = min(lr_max, lr * 1.02)
                if it % 250 == 0:
                    idx = min(len(taus) - 1, idx + 1)
            else:
                stagnant += 1
                lr = max(lr_min, lr * 0.9)
                if stagnant > 12:
                    idx = (idx + 1) % len(taus)
                    stagnant = 5
                if stagnant > 70 and rng is not None:
                    p = perturb(a, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.2)))
                    a = normalize_floor(p)
                    m.fill(0.0)
                    v.fill(0.0)
                    step = 0
                    stagnant = 0
                    lr = min(lr_max, lr * 1.15)
                    idx = max(0, idx - 1)
        else:
            stagnant += 10
            if stagnant > 40 and rng is not None:
                p = perturb(a, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.15)))
                a = normalize_floor(p)
                m.fill(0.0)
                v.fill(0.0)
                step = 0
                stagnant = 0
                idx = max(0, idx - 1)

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def top_uniform_and_ratio_weights(c, e):
    weights = []
    for beta in (2.0, 10.0, 50.0, 300.0, 2000.0, 20000.0):
        weights.append(ratio_weights(e, beta))
    for k in (1, 3, 8, 24, 80):
        weights.append(top_uniform_weights(c, k))
    weights.append(excess_weights(c, max(16, min(80, c.size // 64))))
    return weights


def active_subgrad_sweep(a, deadline, verbose=False, tag=""):
    """
    Deterministic fine polish: systematically try several active weights and steps.
    """
    a = normalize_floor(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float(best_sc), best_a

    rounds = 0
    max_rounds = 4
    while rounds < max_rounds and time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        rounds += 1
        c = fast_self_conv(best_a)
        e = 2.0 * float(n) * c
        improved = False
        for w in top_uniform_and_ratio_weights(c, e):
            h = corr_pair(best_a, w)
            hc = h - float(np.mean(h))
            hc = np.nan_to_num(hc, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            hscale = float(np.max(hc) - np.min(hc))
            if hscale <= 1e-25:
                continue
            for eta in (0.01, 0.02, 0.05, 0.1, 0.2):
                a_try = multiplicative_delta_update(best_a, -eta * hc / hscale)
                sc_try = exact_score(a_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-13:
                    best_sc = sc_try
                    best_a = a_try
                    improved = True
        if not improved:
            break
        if verbose and rounds % 2 == 0:
            print("  [{} sub r{}] score={:.10f}".format(tag, rounds, best_sc))
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.nan_to_num(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel(),
                      nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, a).astype(np.float64)


def to_length_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == int(n):
        return normalize_floor(a)
    return normalize_floor(resample_sequence(a, n))


def add_init_to_list(inits, arr, n, cap):
    if len(inits) >= int(cap):
        return inits
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    elif arr.size != int(n):
        arr = resample_sequence(arr, n)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    mn = float(np.min(arr))
    if mn < 0:
        arr = arr - mn + 0.01
    if float(arr.sum()) <= 0:
        arr = np.ones(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    inits.append(normalize_a(arr))
    return inits


def generate_inits(n, rng, parent=None, cap=30):
    inits = []
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    if parent is not None:
        p = resample_sequence(parent, n)
        add_init_to_list(inits, p, n, cap)
        add_init_to_list(inits, p[::-1], n, cap)

    add_init_to_list(inits, np.ones(n), n, cap)

    # Center-low / center-high shapes.
    for alpha in (0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 1.8, 2.5):
        add_init_to_list(inits, 1.0 - alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2, n, cap)
        add_init_to_list(inits, 1.0 + alpha * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2, n, cap)

    # Cosine waves.
    for f in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6):
        for alpha in (0.25, 0.5, 0.9, 1.5):
            add_init_to_list(inits, 1.0 + alpha * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * x), n, cap)

    # Absolute sine.
    for f in (1, 2, 3):
        for alpha in (0.5, 1.0, 1.8):
            add_init_to_list(inits, 0.4 + alpha * np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * f * x)), n, cap)

    # Exponential tapers.
    for rate in (1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0, 8.0, 12.0):
        add_init_to_list(inits, np.exp(-rate * x) + np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)) + 0.02, n, cap)
        add_init_to_list(inits, np.exp(-rate * np.abs(x - 0.5)) + 0.02, n, cap)
        add_init_to_list(inits, np.exp(-rate * x) + 0.05, n, cap)
        add_init_to_list(inits, np.exp(-rate * (1.0 - x)) + 0.05, n, cap)

    # Bimodal.
    for width in (0.04, 0.08, 0.12, 0.18):
        for center in (0.2, 0.32, 0.45):
            add_init_to_list(inits,
                             np.exp(-(((x - center) / width) ** 2)) +
                             np.exp(-(((x - (1.0 - center)) / width) ** 2)) +
                             0.02,
                             n, cap)

    # Power tents.
    for p in (0.4, 0.8, 1.2, 2.0, 3.5):
        add_init_to_list(inits, (1.0 - np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0)) ** p + 0.02, n, cap)

    # Smooth random spectra.
    Nf = next_power_of_two(n)
    for _ in range(4):
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf) * Nf
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.3, 3.5)))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        add_init_to_list(inits, 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.0)) * noise, n, cap)

    # Raw random.
    for _ in range(3):
        add_init_to_list(inits, 0.05 + 0.95 * rng.random(n), n, cap)

    if not inits:
        inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)
    return inits


def best_initial_sequence(n, rng, parent=None, cap=25):
    inits = generate_inits(n, rng, parent, cap)
    best_a = inits[0]
    best_sc = exact_score(best_a)
    for a in inits[1:]:
        sc = exact_score(a)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a
    return float(best_sc), best_a


def perturb(a, rng, strength=None):
    p = normalize_floor(a)
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return p
    if strength is None:
        strength = float(rng.uniform(0.04, 0.22))
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 9))

    if mode == 0:
        q = p * np.exp(strength * rng.standard_normal(n))

    elif mode == 1:
        t = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.3))
        q = (1.0 - t) * p + t * (1.0 / float(n))

    elif mode == 2:
        q = p[::-1] * np.exp(0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n))

    elif mode == 3:
        # Gaussian smoothing.
        k = int(rng.integers(3, max(5, min(n, n // 100 if n > 100 else n))))
        if k % 2 == 0:
            k += 1
        if k >= n:
            k = n if n % 2 == 1 else max(3, n - 1)
        if k < 3:
            k = 3
        c = k // 2
        sig = max(1.0, c / 2.0)
        kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
        kern /= kern.sum()
        q = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")

    elif mode == 4:
        # V-shape perturbation.
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        q = p * (1.0 - strength * 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    elif mode == 5:
        # U-shape perturbation.
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        q = p * (1.0 + strength * 0.5 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)

    elif mode == 6:
        # Low-frequency random smooth multiplicative noise.
        Nf = next_power_of_two(n)
        freqs = np.fft.rfftfreq(Nf)
        power = 1.0 / ((np.abs(freqs) + 1.0) ** float(rng.uniform(1.5, 3.0)))
        power[0] = 0.0
        phase = rng.uniform(-np.pi, np.pi, size=power.size)
        spec = power * np.exp(1j * phase)
        spec[0] = 0.0
        noise = np.fft.irfft(spec, Nf)[:n]
        noise = noise - float(noise.mean())
        q = p * np.exp(strength * 0.35 * noise)

    elif mode == 7:
        # Block modulation.
        nb = int(rng.integers(4, max(5, n // 80)))
        per = max(1, n // nb)
        block = 1.0 + strength * 0.35 * (rng.random(nb) * 2.0 - 1.0)
        q = p * np.repeat(block, per)
        if q.size < n:
            q = np.concatenate([q, np.full(n - q.size, q[-1])])
        elif q.size > n:
            q = q[:n]

    else:
        q = p * (0.85 + 0.3 * rng.random(n))

    q = np.nan_to_num(q, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
    return normalize_floor(q)


def flatten_c(c, t):
    """
    Target convolution with peaks capped near t, then rescaled to sum 1.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.maximum(np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0), 0.0)
    t = float(max(0.0, t))
    over = np.maximum(c - t, 0.0)
    out = c - over
    deficit = 1.0 - float(out.sum())
    if deficit > 0.0:
        out = out + deficit / float(out.size)
    elif deficit < -1e-12:
        under = np.maximum(t - out, 0.0)
        us = float(under.sum())
        if us > 0.0:
            out = out - (-deficit) * under / us
        else:
            out = out - (-deficit) / float(out.size)
    out = np.maximum(out, 1e-20)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return out / s


def spectral_square_root(c, n):
    """
    Attempt a frequency-domain square root: if C_hat = DFT(c), choose
    A = sqrt(C_hat) and take IFFT.  Branch is principal; both signs are tried
    by the caller.
    """
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = 2 * int(n) - 1
    if c.size < m:
        c_pad = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        c_pad[:c.size] = c
    else:
        c_pad = c[:m].astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    N = next_power_of_two(m)
    C = np.fft.rfft(c_pad, N)
    A = np.sqrt(C)
    if A.size > 0:
        A[0] = complex(abs(A[0].real), 0.0)
    if A.size > 1 and (N % 2 == 0):
        A[-1] = complex(A[-1].real, 0.0)
    a = np.fft.irfft(A, N)[:n]
    return np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def score_raw_root(arr_raw):
    """
    Try positive part and shifted version of a raw square-root sequence.
    """
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    if arr_raw.size == 0:
        return best_sc, best_a
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr_raw)):
        return best_sc, best_a

    for arr in (np.clip(arr_raw, 0.0, None), arr_raw - float(np.min(arr_raw)) + 1e-12):
        if arr.size == 0:
            continue
        s = float(arr.sum())
        if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            continue
        a_try = normalize_floor(arr)
        sc = exact_score(a_try)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a_try
    return best_sc, best_a


def spectral_flatten_candidates(a, base_sc, deadline, rng, verbose=True):
    """
    Generate constrained-looking convolution targets (capped / smoothed / skewed)
    and project them back to sequences via frequency-domain square roots.
    This is used primarily as a diversification / restart mechanism.
    """
    a0 = normalize_floor(a)
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = exact_score(a0)
    n = a0.size
    if n == 0:
        return float(best_sc), best_a

    m = 2 * n - 1
    base = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64) / float(m)
    c = fast_self_conv(a0)
    c = np.nan_to_num(c, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    cs = float(c.sum())
    if cs <= 0.0:
        return float(best_sc), best_a
    c = c / cs
    cmax = float(np.max(c))

    count = 0
    max_count = 1200
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0 and count < max_count:
        count += 1
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 8))

        if mode == 0:
            t = cmax * float(rng.uniform(0.90, 0.995))
            c_new = flatten_c(c, t)
        elif mode == 1:
            t = cmax * float(rng.uniform(0.95, 0.999))
            c_new = flatten_c(c, t)
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.4))
            c_new = (1.0 - alpha) * c_new + alpha * base
        elif mode == 2:
            alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
            c_new = (1.0 - alpha) * c + alpha * base
        elif mode == 3:
            k = int(rng.integers(11, 51))
            if k % 2 == 0:
                k += 1
            c0 = k // 2
            sig = max(1.0, k / 4.0)
            kern = np.exp(-((np.arange(k) - c0) ** 2) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
            kern /= kern.sum()
            c_new = np.convolve(c, kern, mode="same")
        elif mode == 4:
            x = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
            kappa = float(rng.uniform(-5.0, 5.0))
            gamma = float(rng.uniform(-3.0, 3.0))
            c_new = np.exp(kappa * (2.0 * x - 1.0) + gamma * x) + 0.05
        elif mode == 5:
            x = (np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(m)
            freq = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.1, 0.6))
            c_new = 1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x) + 0.15
        elif mode == 6:
            c_new = c * (1.0 + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(m))
        else:
            t = cmax * float(rng.uniform(0.97, 0.999))
            c_new = flatten_c(c, t)

        c_new = np.nan_to_num(c_new, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        c_new = np.maximum(c_new, 1e-20)
        s = float(c_new.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            continue
        c_new = c_new / s

        raw = spectral_square_root(c_new, n)
        for raw_s in (raw, -raw):
            sc, a_try = score_raw_root(raw_s)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = a_try.copy()

        if verbose and count % 200 == 0 and np.isfinite(best_sc):
            print("  [spec {}] count={} best={:.10f} base={:.10f}".format(
                "spec", count, best_sc, base_sc))

    return float(best_sc), best_a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point.  Returns a list of non-negative floats minimizing the score.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    deadline = t_start + budget - 15.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Load parent if available.
    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            p = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            p = np.nan_to_num(p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if p.size >= 16 and float(p.sum()) > 0.01:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    # Main high-resolution length.  16384 keeps self-convolution FFT at 32768
    # while giving the finest grid within that FFT size.
    n_main = 16384

    # Global best of any length (parent may remain if it is still best).
    global_a = None
    global_sc = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        pnorm = normalize_floor(parent)
        psc = exact_score(pnorm)
        if np.isfinite(psc):
            global_a = pnorm.copy()
            global_sc = float(psc)

    # ----- Low-resolution phase -------------------------------------------
    if parent is not None:
        n_low = int(parent.size)
        if n_low < 4096:
            n_low = 8192
        elif n_low > 12288:
            n_low = 10240
    else:
        n_low = 8192

    if parent is not None:
        low_a = to_length_sequence(parent, n_low)
        low_sc = exact_score(low_a)
    else:
        low_sc, low_a = best_initial_sequence(n_low, rng, None, cap=20)

    if np.isfinite(low_sc) and low_sc < global_sc:
        global_sc = low_sc
        global_a = low_a.copy()

    # Low mirror / pressure search.
    low_mirror_dead = min(deadline, t_start + 0.20 * budget)
    try:
        if low_mirror_dead > time.monotonic() + 15:
            sc, seq = mirror_optimize(low_a, low_mirror_dead, rng=rng,
                                      verbose=True, tag="low1")
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                low_sc = sc
                low_a = seq
                if sc < global_sc:
                    global_sc = sc
                    global_a = seq.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Low LSE-LBFGS polish.
    low_lse_dead = min(deadline, t_start + 0.32 * budget)
    try:
        if low_lse_dead > time.monotonic() + 15:
            sc, seq = lse_lbfgs_run(low_a, low_lse_dead, verbose=True, tag="low2")
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                low_sc = sc
                low_a = seq
                if sc < global_sc:
                    global_sc = sc
                    global_a = seq.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # ----- High-resolution phase ------------------------------------------
    if parent is not None:
        high_a = to_length_sequence(global_a, n_main)
    else:
        high_sc_init, high_a = best_initial_sequence(n_main, rng, None, cap=30)
        high_a = to_length_sequence(high_a, n_main)
    high_sc = exact_score(high_a)
    if np.isfinite(high_sc) and high_sc < global_sc:
        global_sc = high_sc
        global_a = high_a.copy()

    # High mirror / pressure search.
    high_mirror_dead = min(deadline, t_start + 0.55 * budget)
    try:
        if high_mirror_dead > time.monotonic() + 20:
            sc, seq = mirror_optimize(high_a, high_mirror_dead, rng=rng,
                                      verbose=True, tag="hi1")
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                high_sc = sc
                high_a = seq
                if sc < global_sc:
                    global_sc = sc
                    global_a = seq.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # High LSE-LBFGS polish from the best available basin.
    high_lse_dead = min(deadline, t_start + 0.78 * budget)
    try:
        if high_lse_dead > time.monotonic() + 25:
            base_high = to_length_sequence(global_a, n_main)
            sc, seq = lse_lbfgs_run(base_high, high_lse_dead, verbose=True, tag="hi2")
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                high_sc = sc
                high_a = seq
                if sc < global_sc:
                    global_sc = sc
                    global_a = seq.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Spectral square-root diversification at high resolution.
    spec_dead = min(deadline, t_start + 0.85 * budget)
    try:
        if spec_dead > time.monotonic() + 60:
            base_spec = to_length_sequence(global_a, n_main)
            sc, seq = spectral_flatten_candidates(base_spec, global_sc,
                                                  spec_dead, rng, verbose=True)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and seq is not None:
                if sc < high_sc:
                    high_sc = sc
                    high_a = seq.copy()
                if sc < global_sc:
                    global_sc = sc
                    global_a = seq.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Short restart / diversification moves.
    restart_dead = min(deadline, t_start + 0.94 * budget)
    restarts = 0
    try:
        while time.monotonic() < restart_dead - 20 and restarts < 5:
            rem = restart_dead - 20.0 - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 45:
                break
            base = to_length_sequence(global_a, n_main)
            p = perturb(base, rng, strength=float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.20)))
            chunk = min(rem * 0.6, 120.0)
            sc, seq = mirror_optimize(p, time.monotonic() + chunk, rng=rng,
                                      verbose=False, tag=("r" + str(restarts)),
                                      tau_idx=9)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                if sc < high_sc:
                    high_sc = sc
                    high_a = seq.copy()
                if sc < global_sc:
                    global_sc = sc
                    global_a = seq.copy()
            restarts += 1
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Final deterministic subgradient polish on the current global best.
    try:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 10 and global_a is not None:
            sc, seq = active_subgrad_sweep(global_a, deadline - 5,
                                           verbose=True, tag="final")
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < global_sc:
                global_sc = sc
                global_a = seq.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # If high-resolution work eventually beat the global best, ensure it is used.
    try:
        if high_a is not None:
            hs = exact_score(high_a)
            if np.isfinite(hs) and hs < global_sc:
                global_sc = hs
                global_a = high_a.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Cleanup and return.
    try:
        if global_a is None:
            global_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
        out_arr = np.asarray(global_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        out_arr = np.nan_to_num(out_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(out_arr.sum())
        if out_arr.size == 0 or s < 0.01:
            out_arr = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5
            s = float(out_arr.sum())
        out_arr = out_arr / s
        out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        out = [float(x) for x in out_arr]
        if sum(out) < 0.01:
            out = [0.5] * 1024
        print("DONE internal={:.10f} n={} time={:.1f}s".format(
            global_sc, len(out), time.monotonic() - t_start))
        return out
    except Exception:
        # Extremely defensive fallback.
        return [0.5] * 1024
```
